Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 150 is immediate from the factorization result property. For the inductive step, we decompose the transcription structure using the factorization result theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 626. Each subproblem has a solution by the factorization result fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
